- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

(part 95/96)

# Relics
Aliases: Relic, Relic Hunting, Relic hunting
A relic underground in the [[Spider's Den]].
Relics are collectables similar to [[Fairy Souls]] and are hidden around parts of the [[Spider's Den]]. There are 28 in total, and discovering a new relic gives the player 10,000 [[Coins]]. Upon discovering all 28 relics, the [[Archaeologist]] will give the player an extra 30,000 [[Coins]] for completing his [[Quests|quest]].
When interacting with the [[Archaeologist]] before finding any of the relics, he gives out the [[Archaeologist's Compass]] which points in the direction of the nearest Relic.
## Relic Locations
The tables below index the exact coordinate locations of all 28 relic, all of which are found within the [[Spider's Den]].
[[Coordinates]] can be viewed by pressing F3 on the keyboard, or by using a mod that has coordinate display.
Note: Click on the rows to keep track of the Relics collected. This persists even after reloading or refreshing the page.:

# Rift Guide
Aliases: Rift Armor, Rift armor
The Rift Guide is an in-game guide on how the player should progress in each section of the [[Rift Dimension]]. To unlock the Rift Guide, you must speak with the [[Wizard But Weird]] at the top of the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]].
The Rift Guide is accessible through the [[SkyBlock Menu]] or through the command /riftguide while inside the Rift.
## Rift Guide
Home of the Argofays. No one knows how they got here, but they do like visits from strangers!:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Craft the Crux Talisman|Defeat Shies and craft their cruxes into the Crux Talisman.|You must kill Shies ([[Shy]]) and obtain Shy Cruxes ([[Shy Crux]]) to craft the [[Crux Talisman]].|
|Reed's Boat: Powerful Glue|Acquire materials from the Wyld Woods to complete Reed's boat.|You must collect 16x [[Larva Silk]] and give it to [[Reed]].|
|Help out Bughunter|Help out the hungry Argofay Bughunter looking to "collect" Odonatas.|You must collect 8x [[Bottled Odonata]] and give it to [[Argofay Bughunter]].|
|Montezuma's First|There is a fragment of Montezuma somewhere in the Wyld Woods.|The first fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found under a tree in the Wyld Woods.|
|Montezuma's Second|There is a fragment of Montezuma somewhere in the Rift Gallery.|The second fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found in a house in the Rift Gallery, opened with a stone button.|
|Eye: The Intruder|Find Porhtal's eye in the Wizard Tower.|Find and calm [[Porhtal]]'s first eye in the Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]]), allowing you to fast travel to the Wizard Tower.|
|Help Inverted Sirius|Inverted Sirius found a way to transfer items in-and-out of the Rift, but he's in a bit of a predicament.; He's "standing" behind his shack.|Talk to the many customers in the [[Shifted Tavern]] to obtain the [[Stability Elixir]] from the [[Argofay Serialbather]], which you can give to [[Inverted Sirius]] to gain access to Rift-Transferable items.|
|Talk to Sad Jacquelle|Sad Jacquelle needs help to find her cat. She's on a branch next to her house in the Wyld Woods.|Talk to [[Sad Jacquelle]], who will give you the [[Montezuma Pet]]. Finding Montezuma Soul Fragments will upgrade Montezuma.|
|Talk to Porhtal|Porhtal knows a trick to fast travel in the Rift.|Talk to [[Porhtal]], who will let you calm his many eyes in the Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]]), [[Lagoon Hut]], [[Dreadfarm]], [[Village Plaza]], Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]]), [[Stillgore Château]], and [[The Mountaintop]].|
|Wear Enigma's Cloak|Talk to Enigma at the base of the Wizard Tower and wear the Enigma's Cloak.|Talk to Enigma at the base of the Wizard Tower and wear the [[Enigma Cloak]].|
|Talk to the Threebrothers|Three argofays have a spare weapon hidden in the trees.|Talk to all three Argofay Threebrothers ([[Argofay Threebrother]]) and purchase the [[Wyld Sword]].|
|Wear the Wyld Armor Set|Obtain the armor set from the Argofay Traffickers chilling in the treehouses.|Purchase a full set of [[Wyld Armor]] from the Argofay Traffickers ([[Argofay Trafficker]]).|
Thin strip of land and yet very dangerous. Mushrooms pop out when detecting nearby vibrations.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Montezuma's Third|There is a fragment of Montezuma behind a waterfall, obviously.|The third fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found behind a waterfall in the entrance leading to the [[Leeches Lair]].|
|Slay Deadgehogs|Craft a helmet using spines obtained from killing deadgehogs.|Craft the [[Not-Dead-Gehog Mask]] using a Wyld Helmet on a Helmet ([[Wyld Armor]]) and Deadgehog Spines ([[Deadgehog Spine]]).|
|Defeat the Leech Supreme|Help out the scientific community in the lagoon with some fantastic research.|Defeat [[Leech Supreme]] in the [[Leeches Lair]].; Leads to a timecharm...|
|Eye: The Gill-Man|Find Porhtal's eye in the Lagoon Hut.|Find and calm [[Porhtal]]'s second eye in the [[Lagoon Hut]], allowing you to fast travel to the basement.|
|Talk to Reed|Reed is stranded on the Lagoon Hut and it's not because he created a special profile.|Talk to [[Reed]], who will allow you to purchase the [[Rift Boat]] after providing him with the necessary ingredients.|
As the focal point of study of Dr. Emmett, the west end of the village is where reality is at its most twisted.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Craft the Crux Ring|Defeat Shadows and craft their cruxes into the Crux Ring.|You must kill Shadows ([[Shadow]]) and obtain Shadow Cruxes ([[Shadow Crux]]) to craft the [[Crux Ring]].|
|Complete the Mirrorverse|Follow Dr. Emmett's study through his mirror puzzles and experiments.|Complete all seven rooms of the [[Mirrorverse]].; Leads to a timecharm...|
|Ward against the fireball|Sorcerer Okron is holding a giant fireball still. Collect glyphs and cast warding spells to protect the house.|Help [[Sorcerer Okron]] with collecting glyphs scattered around the [[West Village]] and [[Dreadfarm]] and using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to cast the spells.|
|Montezuma's Fourth|There is a fragment of Montezuma stashed on a roof in the West Village.|The fourth fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found on top of the roof of the building Plumber Joe ([[Plumber Joe (Rift)]]) is in.|
|Cleanup the Infested House|Do Kat's job for her, fill the vermin bin and obtain the disinfestor gloves.|Obtain the [[Disinfestor Gloves]] from the [[Infested House]] by filling up the Vermin Bin.|
|Hacking for dummies|Some unhinged kloon wants to hack the world.|Hack terminals across the [[West Village]] and obtain the [[Defective Monitor]] from [[Unhinged Kloon]].|
|Gunther's Race|Complete the race in the West Village and upgrade your boots.|Complete [[Gunther]]'s [[Rift Race]] and craft the [[Gunther's Sneakers]].|
Fertilized by a long-gone necromancer, the dreadfarm grows crops unique to this dimension.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Craft the Crux Artifact|Defeat Volts and craft their cruxes into the Crux Artifact.|You must kill Volts ([[Volt]]) and obtain Volt Cruxes ([[Volt Crux]]) to craft the [[Crux Artifact]].|
|Reed's Boat: Replacement Wood|Acquire materials from the Dreadfarm to complete Reed's boat.|You must collect 64x [[Wilted Berberis]] and give it to [[Reed]].|
|Montezuma's Fifth|There is a fragment of Montezuma behind a breakable wall in the Dreadfarm.|The fifth fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found behind a weak wall in the [[Dreadfarm]], which can be blown up by a [[Horsezooka]].|
|Eye: The Baba Yaga|Find Porhtal's eye in the Dreadfarm.|Find and calm [[Porhtal]]'s third eye in the [[Dreadfarm]], allowing you to fast travel to the farm.|
|Harvest for Shania|Collect all the resources that Shania needs. This task completes once you wear Chicken Leggs!|Craft and wear the [[Chicken Leggs]].; Leads to a timecharm...|
Whatever marvels you find in the village center, be sure to avoid the endermen roaming in it.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Craft the Crux Relic|Defeat Scribes and craft their cruxes into the Crux Relic.|You must kill Scribes ([[Scribe]]) and obtain Scribe Cruxes ([[Scribe Crux]]) to craft the [[Crux Relic]].|
|Barry, Premier Leader of SkyBlock|Deal with the protestors stressing out Barry about their needs and wants.|Help [[Barry]] deal with the p ([[Creed]])r ([[Dust]])o ([[Frankie]])t ([[Harriette]])e ([[Soma]])st ([[Stain]])ors ([[Violetta]]).; Leads to a timecharm...|
|Shen Auctions|Damia is running auctions for Shen in the Rift Auction House. Make a bid on an item!|Make a bid on an item in [[Barrier Street]].|
|Reed's Boat: Tasty Provisions|Acquire materials from the Village Plaza to complete Reed's boat.|You must give 128x [[Half-Eaten Carrot]] to [[Reed]].|
|Cowboy Nick's Rabbits|Feed Cowboy Nick's hungry rabbits with carrots.|Collect Half-Eaten Carrots ([[Half-Eaten Carrot]]) in the [[Half-Eaten Cave]] and craft 7x [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]] for [[Cowboy Nick]].|
|Montezuma's Sixth|There is a fragment of Montezuma inside a book in the plaza.|The sixth fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found in the [[Book in a Book]], which can be accessed from clicking a bookshelf in [[Marcia]]'s Flower House.|
|Montezuma's Seventh|There is a fragment of Montezuma stashed on an islet in the déjà vu alley.|The seventh fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found on a floating island next to the [[Déjà Vu Alley]].|
|Reverse-Murder Investigation|Assist Detective Amog in solving a murder case that is about to happen.|Help [[Detective Amog]] in solving the planned murder of [[Ramero]], in which you yourself are the murderer.|
|Eye: The Bankster|Find Porhtal's eye in the Village Plaza.|Find and calm [[Porhtal]]'s fourth eye in the [[Village Plaza]], allowing you to fast travel to the plaza.|
|Seraphine's Horrible Face|Seraphine was detransfigured when she attempted to cross into the rift.|Obtain the [[Anti-Morph Potion]] to help [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] fix her face and obtain the [[Used Detransfigured Face]].|
|Fancy Sneakers|Purchase good shoes from Seymour at the tailor's house. They last for life!|Purchase the [[Exceedingly Comfy Sneakers]].|
The only place where staring at walls may be interesting, because they're as much alive as you are.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Craft the Crux Heirloom|Defeat Frozilles and craft their cruxes into the Crux Heirloom.|You must kill Frozilles ([[Frozille]]) and obtain Frosty Cruxes ([[Frosty Crux]]) to craft the [[Crux Heirloom]].|
|Reed's Boat: Living Anchor|Acquire materials from the Living Cave to complete Reed's boat.|You must craft a [[Living Metal Anchor]] and give it to [[Reed]].|
|Craft Self-Recursive Pickaxe|Defeat Autonulls and use Nullified Metal to upgrade your sword into... a pickaxe.|Craft a [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]] using [[Nullified Metal]].|
|Max out your suit|Keep juicing up your armor pieces until you have the Living Metal Suit bonus on all on them.|Defeat Autocaps ([[Autocap]]), Autochests ([[Autochest]]), [[Autopants]], and [[Autoboots]] to fully juice up your armor and enter the [[Living Stillness]].|
|Craft the Living Timecharm|Jump down to the Living Stillness and defeat Frozilles to craft the Living Timecharm.|Craft the [[Living Timecharm]] using Frosty Cruxes ([[Frosty Crux]]) and [[Living Metal]].; Leads to a timecharm... obviously...|
|Obtain a Living Metal Heart|Talk to the rock denizens of the living stillness to get a lead on how to obtain living metal hearts.|Use the [[Frozen Water Pungi]] to calm the Living Metal Snakes and obtain a [[Living Metal Heart]].|
|Montezuma's Eighth|There is a fragment of Montezuma, under snow in the living stillness.|The eighth fragment of Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) can be found in the [[Living Stillness]] under a breakable snow block.|
A threatening slime has infested the colosseum, its toxicity channeled by everyone's memories of a famous dictator.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|
|Reed's Boat: Powerful Glue|Acquire materials from the Colosseum to complete Reed's boat.|You must give 8x Bacte Fragments ([[Bacte Fragment]]) to [[Reed]].|
|Defeat Bacte|Assemble your friends and defeat the plagued remnants of a dictator who is still trying to infect everyone's life.|Kill [[Bacte]] in the Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]]) and obtain Bacte Fragments ([[Bacte Fragment]]).; Leads to a timecharm...|
|Eye: The Gooey|Find Porhtal's eye in the Colosseum.|Find and calm [[Porhtal]]'s fifth eye in the [[Colosseum]], allowing you to fast travel to the arena.|
A cult of vampires has settled in the castle. Beware of being sent to their prison.:
|Task|Description|Goal|
|-|-|-|

# Roman Numerals
Roman Numerals are numbers used in [[SkyBlock]] to represent the level of a certain [[Skills|Skill]], [[Collections|Collection]], [[Bestiary]], or [[Enchantments|Enchantment]].
A guide to understanding Roman Numerals can be found on its corresponding wikipedia page.
It is possible toggle Roman Numerals in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]'s Settings → Personal → User Interface.
Linked from: [[Enchanting]], [[Farming]], [[Foraging]], [[Runecrafting]], [[Taming]]

# Safari Tickets
Aliases: Basic Safari Ticket, Economy Safari Ticket, First-Class Safari Ticket, Premium Safari Ticket, Safari Ticket
[Safari Tickets]
Safari Tickets are a currency used in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. They are used to access the [[Critter Safari]].
## Obtaining
### Miria's Contest
Safari Tickets can be obtained from [[Miria]]. Higher brackets reward better tickets:
- Basic Safari Ticket: Obtained from reaching the [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] bracket and unlocks the Basic Safari Experience.
- Economy Safari Ticket: Obtained from reaching the [[Rarity|RARE]] bracket and unlocks the Economy Safari Experience.
- Premium Safari Ticket: Obtained from reaching the [[Rarity|EPIC]] bracket and unlocks the Premium Safari Experience.
- First-Class Safari Ticket: Obtained from reaching the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] bracket and unlocks the First-Class Safari Experience.
- Sparkling Safari Ticket [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.): Obtained only from [[Centauri]]. Exclusive for [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] for testing.
### Free Trial
Selecting the Free Trial perk in the [[Heart of the Forest]] allows the player to obtain a Basic Safari Ticket from their first [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]] of the day.
### Ticket Exchange
Higher tier Safari Tickets can be bought at [[Safari Manager]] with lower tier Safari Tickets and [[Forest Essence]].
- Basic Safari Ticket and 250 (3 stacks plus 58) [[Forest Essence]] → Economy Safari Ticket
- Economy Safari Ticket and 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Forest Essence]] → Premium Safari Ticket
- Premium Safari Ticket and 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Forest Essence]] → First-Class Safari Ticket.
## Usage
Safari Tickets allow the player to access the [[Critter Safari]] and gain the following bonuses when entering:
- Basic Safari Ticket: The player starts with 32 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]].
- Economy Safari Ticket: The player starts with 40 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] and gains +10% [[Safari Essence]] and +5 [[Hunting Wisdom]].
- Premium Safari Ticket: The player starts with 48 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] and gains +25% [[Safari Essence]] and +15 [[Hunting Wisdom]].
- First-Class Safari Ticket: The player starts with 56 [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]], gains +50% [[Safari Essence]], +25 [[Hunting Wisdom]], and gains a random Quest Item upon entering the [[Critter Safari]].
- Sparkling Safari Ticket [AHN]: Has same bonuses as First-Class Safari Ticket but also guaranteed Sparkling Critter.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Safari Tickets.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Safari Tickets.|
|Aug 19, 2026|Reduced [[Hunting Wisdom]] granted by Safari Tickets.|

# Shard Families
Aliases: Bird Family, Cave Dweller, Crime, Elemental, Elemental Shard, Elemental Shards, Family, Reptile
[Shard Families]
Shard Families are groups of [[Attributes|Shards]], usually grouped by the shard's mob, that are used to categorize shards for different [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]] recipes.
## Fusion
Specific Shard Families are needed for different [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusions]], usually to obtain higher valued shards of the same family.
The Reptile, Amphibian and Elemental family shards only require 2 shards to combine in the [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion Machine]] instead of the regular 5.
Reptile family shards can also be used with the [[Crocodile Shard|Pure Reptile]] attribute to have an up to +20% chance to gain double shards from the fusion.

# Shiny Orb/Loot
Upon bringing a [[Shiny Pig]] back to a [[Shiny Orb]], the player receives a [[Shiny Token]] and one reward from the table below.
- The [[Pig Brain Mixin]] grants a +10% chance to loot a 2nd item from the loot table below.
- The amount of [[Coins]] can be increased by the following sources, stacking additively:
[[Coins]] Modifiers on [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]]:
|[[Coins]] Increase|Type of Source|Source|
|-|-|-|
|+25%||[[Shiny Rod]]|
|+30%|[[Bestiary]]|Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])|
|+0.20% → 20%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|+0.35% → 35%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|+0.50% → 50%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|Total = +105%|||

# Shiny Pig/Message
This message table is used on [[Shiny Pig]] and [[Shiny Orb]].
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|On Interaction with a Shiny Pig of another player|Oink! This pig has already been clicked by someone else!|
|On Bringing a Shiny Pig to its Orb|SHINY! The orb is charged! Click on it for loot!|
|On Clicking on a Shiny Orb charged by the player|SHINY! You extracted [#]x [item] from the pigglet's orb!|
|On Clicking on a Shiny Orb charged by another player|Only the orb's owner may click on it!|
|When Shiny Pig is not brought to its Orb after 90 seconds|Your Shiny Orb and associated pig expired and disappeared.|
|Condition|Message|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig without holding a Shiny Orb|Oink! Use a Shiny Orb from the Pig Shop to capture the shiny from this piggy!|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig when holding a Shiny Orb when two other Shiny Pigs are active|You may only "own" up to 2 shiny pigs at once.|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig when holding a Shiny Orb otherwise|Oink! Bring the pig back to the Shiny Orb!|

# Shops/Buying
Players can use their [[Coins]] or other [[Currency|Currencies]] and sometimes Materials to purchase items from NPCs at [[Shops]], generally there is a limit of buying the same item 640 times per day from the same NPC. Certain items like most products sold by [[Builder]] have none. The daily limit for can be increased 10x by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk. Items that can purchased from different shops, such as [[Iron Ingot]] from [[Mining Merchant|Mine Merchant]] and [[Iron Forger]] don't share stock, allowing players to purchase up to 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] per day. Despite this, [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] share their stock. The general rule of thumb is:
- Stackable items sold by NPC shops for [[Coins]], that can be traded on [[Bazaar]], have a stock of 640 items.
  - Additionally, this also includes items that cannot be sold on Bazaar, but can be turned into items that can be: [[Block of Quartz]], [[Glowstone]], [[Sugar]] and [[Wet Sponge]].
  - However, the following items don't have a stock [[Netherrack]], [[Wool|White Wool]], [[Experience Bottle]], [[Superboom TNT]] and [[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]].
- Unstackable items sold by NPCs, items sold by NPCs for something other than [[Coins]] (including items sold for [[Coins]] and something else, like [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]]), or stackable items that cannot be sold on Bazaar don't have a stock.
- Items sold for [[Chocolate]], all items sold by [[Kiara]] and [[Sanger]], most items sold by [[Piggy]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Agnes]] and all of the following have their own unique stock: [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Divine Gift|Divine Gift I]]), [[The Art of Peace]], [[Fiesta Flask]], [[Packrat Shard]] and [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]
  - All of these are also not affected by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk.
  - Certain shops contain perks which can only be purchased a specific amount of times in total on a profile.

# Sinful Dice/Dice Trivia
- [[Sinful Dice]], [[Archfiend Dice]] and [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] are one of few items in the game whose texture varies between items. Each has 6 texture variants, one for each side of the die.
  - Unlike all other items, [[Lucky Dice]] only has one. It is also the only Dice that got a new texture in the SkyBlock Resource Pack.
    - Oddly enough, the old sprite of [[Lucky Dice]], which had 5 pips on top is different than the sprite of 5 pips on top of any other Dice item.
    - Despite there being only 6 different sprites of Dice items, there are actually 48 possible variants of shown sides of a standard dice from an angle where 3 sides are shown.
  - When this item has been dropped on the ground and picked back up by a player, or when switched away from and back to in the hotbar, it will change its texture randomly to one of its six texture variants.

# Skill Tree
Aliases: Skill Trees
Skill Trees grant buffs to the player's progression in a particular [[Skills|Skill]] and its most relevant [[Locations]].
[Skill Trees]
Category: Mechanics
## Overview
- They are not shared between [[Profile|Profiles]].
- They are not shared between accounts.
- They are accessed through their command or the [[SkyBlock Menu]] in certain [[Locations]].
- Skill Trees are named Heart of the X, often abbreviated as HotX, where X is a word related to that [[Skills|Skill]].
- Skill Trees are split in Tiers.
- Tiers contain a set of Perks.
- Perks grant buffs. They can sometimes be Item Abilities. One of them is named Center of the X.
- Heart of the X EXP, often abbreviated as HotX EXP is used to advance to the next Tier.
- Tokens of the X are the currency used to unlock Perks, except those named Center of the X.
- Perk Currencies are used to upgrade Perks. They are obtained en masse from resources or tasks related to the Skill Tree.
Comparison of the current Skill Trees:
|Skill Tree|[[Heart of the Mountain]]|[[Heart of the Forest]]|
|Related Skill|[[Mining]]|[[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]]|
|Related Locations|Mining Islands|Foraging Islands|
|Command|/hotm|/hotf|
|No. of Tiers, Perks (Item Abilities)|10, 46 (6)|8, 36 (3)|
|Heart of the X EXP|HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|HotF EXP ([[Heart of the Forest]])|
|Tokens of the X|Tokens of the Mountain ([[Token of the Mountain]])|Tokens of the Forest ([[Token of the Forest]])|
|Perk Currencies|᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]; ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]]|[[Forest Whispers]]; [[Desert Whispers]]|
## Heart of the Mountain
- Unlocking requirements: completing the [[Quests|There are Dwarves?]] quest from [[Rhys]] (and indirectly [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]]).
## Heart of the Forest
- Unlocking requirements: completing the [[Quests|Heart of the Forest]] quest from [[Hina]] (and indirectly [[Foraging|Foraging XII (12)]]).
Linked from: [[Heart of the Forest]], [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Moonglade Marsh]], [[Shop Perk (disambiguation)]]

# Sprayonator/Usage
### Attracting Pests
The Sprayonator/Usage can be used with the appropriate materials to attract certain [[Pests]] to plots. When a spray is active, [[Pests]] are guaranteed to spawn in the plot the player is farming in, or one of the four directly adjacent plots.
The materials below can be used to attract specific pests:
|Material|Attracted Pests|
|-|-|
|[[Compost]]|[[Earthworm]], [[Mosquito]]|
|[[Plant Matter]]|[[Locust]], [[Slug]]|
|[[Dung]]|[[Beetle]], [[Fly]]|
|[[Honey Jar]]|[[Moth]], [[Cricket]]|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|[[Rat]], [[Mite]]|
|[[Jelly]]|[[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], [[Firefly]]|
The weights of the targeted [[Pests]] are increased by +1,000, on top of the base 100 weight.

# Starborn Temples
Aliases: Benefactor, Starborn Temple, Temple Altar, Will
[Starborn Temples]
Starborn Temples are locations where a puzzle must be completed to unlock a Temple Altar for the permanent bonus and an ability to become a Benefactor for the certain temple.
## Temple Altar
After completing a puzzle near or inside the temple, player will unlock the Will bonus and an ability to become a Benefactor for the certain temple.
### Will
When player unlocks a Temple Altar in the Starborn Temple for the first time, they'll gain a permanent stat bonus (named Will), which depends on the temple type:
- [[Forest Temple]] - [[Foraging Fortune]] on [[Galatea]] islands.
- [[Desert Temple]] - [[Hunting Fortune]] on [[Galatea]] islands.
Amount of stats granted is dependent on the amount of Benefactors for the certain temple:
|[[Forest Temple]] Benefactors|[[Forest Temple]] [[Foraging Fortune]]|[[Desert Temple]] Benefactors|[[Desert Temple]] [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|-|-|-|-|
|0|0 [[Foraging Fortune]]|0|0 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|1|1 [[Foraging Fortune]]|1|0.2 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|5|2 [[Foraging Fortune]]|10|0.4 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|10|3 [[Foraging Fortune]]|25|0.6 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|20|4 [[Foraging Fortune]]|50|0.8 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|40|5 [[Foraging Fortune]]|100|1 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|80|6 [[Foraging Fortune]]|250|1.2 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|160|7 [[Foraging Fortune]]|500|1.4 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|250|8 [[Foraging Fortune]]|1,000|1.6 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|500|9 [[Foraging Fortune]]|2,500|1.8 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|1,000|10 [[Foraging Fortune]]|5,000|2 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|2,000|11 [[Foraging Fortune]]|10,000|2.2 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|4,000|12 [[Foraging Fortune]]|25,000|2.4 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|8,000|13 [[Foraging Fortune]]|50,000|2.6 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|15,000|14 [[Foraging Fortune]]|100,000|2.8 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|30,000|15 [[Foraging Fortune]]|250,000|3 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|60,000|16 [[Foraging Fortune]]|500,000|3.2 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|120,000|17 [[Foraging Fortune]]|1,000,000|3.4 [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|200,000|18 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|300,000|19 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|400,000|20 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|500,000|21 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|625,000|22 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|750,000|23 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|875,000|24 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
|1,000,000|25 [[Foraging Fortune]]|||
Formula for the added value between tiers is:
AddedValueBetweenTiers=CurrentBenefactors−BenefactorsNeededForPreviousTierBenefactorsNeededForNextTier−BenefactorsNeededForPreviousTier×ValuePerTier
For example: if there's 1,600 Benefactors for the Forest Temple, player will gain 10 and 0.6 , which results in 10.6 [[Foraging Fortune]].
### Benefactor
Players can also donate to a Starborn in the Temple Altar to become a Benefactor and unlock a temporary boost:
|Temple|Cost Per Day|Benefactor Bonus|
|-|-|-|
|[[Forest Temple]]|70,000 [[Coins]] 2x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|[[Axes]] you throw are 25% larger and 25% faster.; Grants 2x [[Foraging Fortune]] from the Will bonus.|
|[[Desert Temple]]|70,000 [[Coins]] 2x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Your [[Black Holes]] and [[Huntraps]] work 25% faster.; Grants 2x [[Hunting Fortune]] from the Will bonus.|
Player can only be a Benefactor for one temple at the time. Upon donating, the player will lose their status as a Benefactor of the other temple if they were one, however, the time that this donation would have lasted will not be lost and rather added to the time as current temple Benefactor.
## History

|Jul 10, 2026|Added [[Desert Temple]].|

# Stash
Aliases: Item Stash, Material Stash
[Stash]
Category: Mechanics
The Stash is a reserved "item limbo" where all items go if the player does not have enough inventory space to pick them up.
## Mechanics
Items that cannot fit in the player's inventory are automatically placed into either the Item or Material Stash depending on the type of the item.
- The Item Stash holds up to 720 non-stackable items, or 84 in the [[Rift Dimension]]. Items that bypass this limit are deleted instead of being added to the Stash.
- The Material Stash can hold an infinite amount of stackable items.
If there are items in either Stash, messages will be sent in chat every minute to inform the player. Items can be taken out of their Stash by using the [[Commands|/pickupstash]] command or by pressing the chat message button. The player can also view the contents of either Stash by using the [[Commands|/viewstash]] command (except in the Rift Dimension), or clear all of it by using the [[Commands|/clearstash]] command.
If the player uses [[Commands|/coopsalvage]] to retrieve the items of another player who has left or been kicked from their co-op, the items will go in the Stash if they don't have enough inventory space. This bypasses the normal Item Stash limit.
## Interface
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Item Stash notification (sent every minute)|You have [#] item(s) stashed away! >>> CLICK HERE to pick <it/them> up! <<<|
|Material Stash notification (sent every minute)|You have [#] material(s) stashed away! (This totals [#] type(s) of material stashed) >>> CLICK HERE to pick <it/them> up! <<<|
|An item is added to a Stash|One or more items didn't fit in your inventory and were added to your [type] stash! Click here to pick them up!|
|Picking up some items from Item Stash|From stash: [Item] You still have [#] items in there!|
|Picking up some items from Material Stash|From stash: [Item] You picked up [#] items from your material stash! You still have [#] materials totalling [#] types of materials in there!|
|Picking up all items from a Stash|From stash: [Item] You picked up all items from your [type] stash!|
|Trying to pick up stash items with a full inventory|Couldn't unstash your [type] stash! Your inventory is full!|
|Inserting stash items into Sacks|You have successfully transferred your items from this stash to your sacks!|
|Trying to insert stash items into Sacks without any possible Sacks|You cannot transfer anything into sacks!|
|Using /clearstash|WARNING: This action is irreversible and deletes all [#] stashed items. If you still wish to continue, click below. CLEAR STASH YES I AM SURE|
|Confirming /clearstash|Your stash has been cleared!|
|Using /clearstash with an empty Stash|Your stash is already empty!|
|Item Stash ≥90% full|YOUR STASH IS ALMOST AT MAX CAPACITY!|
|Item Stash completely full|YOUR STASH IS AT MAX CAPACITY! ANY ITEMS SENT TO YOUR STASH WILL BE DELETED|
|Items deleted due to a full Item Stash|Uh oh! [#] item(s) couldn't be stashed because you hit the item stash limit!|
## Tips
- The Stash offers a solution to the storage problem plaguing the [[Rift Dimension]] : players can force redirect items such as [[Coven Seal|Coven Seals]].
## Trivia
- The Stash is likely inspired from The Pit's Stash feature.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Item Stash introduced.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added the ability to view items in the Stash in chat (/viewstash ([[Commands]])) and clear the Stash (/clearstash ([[Commands]])).|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Salvaging ([[Co-op]]) a former co-op member's items will now go into the Stash.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added more messages when items are in the Stash.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Added the Material Stash.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Diversified the chat messages to account for both stashes.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Fixed a bug where [[Minion Storage]] did not go into the player's Stash.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Added UI for /viewstash ([[Commands]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Added the ability to insta-sell the Material Stash to the Bazaar ([[Bazaar (NPC)]]).|
|Mar 18, 2024|Fixed a stash message appearing as a guest on a [[Private Island]].|
|Sep 19, 2024|Fixed [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Smelting Touch]] not applying to items when sent to the Stash.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Fixed a bug where [[Kat]] upgrades would delete Pets sent to the Stash.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Removed "Sell Stash Now" button on Ⓑ Bingo ([[Bingo]]), ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) and ☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) profiles.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Removed the ability to put items from [[Kuudra]] battles into the Stash.|
|Mar 4, 2026|Fixed items not counting towards [[Commissions]] when sent to the Stash.|

Either 1 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] or 1 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] is guaranteed to drop from [[Slug]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Raw Mutton]]|Dropped by Sheep ([[Stranded]]).|

More can be obtained from the [[Nether Wart Crystal]] on the [[Nether Wart Island]], which requires Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]]) to craft. ▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Seeds]] ×4 + [[Fermented Spider Eye]] → [[Nether Wart]] 3 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Beetle]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Potato]]|1% chance to be dropped by Zombies ([[Stranded]]). They can then be planted on farmland. 3 [[Enchanted Potato]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Locust]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Pumpkin]]|Dropped by some Spooky Mobs ([[Spooky]]) and Spooky Sea Creatures ([[Fishing]]) during the [[Spooky Festival]]. Pumpkins can be placed in the crafting grid to turn them into pumpkin seeds, which can then be planted. 1 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Rat]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot. A Rat Pest is guaranteed to spawn during the Pest tutorial at Garden Level V (5).|
|[[Raw Chicken]]|Dropped by Chickens ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Raw Porkchop]]|Dropped by Pigs ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Raw Rabbit]]|Dropped by Rabbits ([[Stranded]]).|

Seeds can be planted to grow Wheat.|

Prior to reaching Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]]), the Desert Island can be obtained from Villagers ([[Stranded]]). 2 [[Enchanted Sugar]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Mosquito]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|

[Mining Collections]
|[[Coal]]|There is a single coal ore at 7, 93, 36 ([[Coordinates]]) on the island that has Jerry. Using 1 Coal to craft an [[Auto Smelter]] allows any Foraging Minion to make Coal. There is a Trade ([[Trades]]) for Coal, which has no unlock requirements, but it doesn't increase the collection. Wood ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) can be smelted in a furnace to make Charcoal, but it doesn't increase the collection. Coal is also a drop from Lava Fishing ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Cobblestone]]|Obtained by mining Stone. Can be obtained in large amounts by making a Cobblestone generator using the materials in the chest on the island that has Jerry. There is also a [[Cobblestone Minion]] that starts on the island.|
|[[Diamond]]|Dropped by Charged Creepers ([[Stranded]]). Harvested by Revenant Minions ([[Revenant Minion]]).|
|[[Emerald]]|Obtained by trading with Villagers ([[Stranded]]). Note that Emerald Minions ([[Emerald Minion]]) are 40 times slower on Stranded profiles.|
|[[End Stone]]|Dropped by Zealots ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Gemstone]]|Some types are dropped by Crystal Hollows Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).|
|[[Glowstone Dust]]|Dropped by Witches ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Gold Ingot]]|Dropped by Redstone Pigmen ([[Stranded]]). Dropped by Golden Ghouls ([[Stranded]]) (requires Combat XXIV (24) ([[Combat]])).|
|[[Gravel]]|Obtained by using a TNT explosion to destroy Stone. Both naturally-occurring Stone and manually placed Stone can drop Gravel. Sometimes Flint drops instead of Gravel.|
|[[Hard Stone]]|Dropped by Water Worms ([[Water Worm]]).|
|[[Ice]]|Obtained by using any tool with [[Silk Touch]] to mine Ice, which is formed by letting water freeze in areas where the biome was changed with a Taiga Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) (requires Spruce Log IV (4) ([[Spruce Log]])). Dropped by some Winter Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]). Obtained by fishing with the [[Ice Rod]], which has a chance to drop from [[Frozen Steve]].|
|[[Iron Ingot]]|Dropped by Iron Golems ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|Dropped by Lapis Zombies ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Mithril]]|Dropped by Star Sentrys ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Mycelium]]|Purchased from a Villager Trade ([[Stranded]]) for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. The item can be purchased from the following Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]), but it does not count towards the collection. Trade: 1 [[Dirt]], 1 Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), and 1 Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) → 1 [[Mycelium]] (requires Mushroom II (2) ([[Mushroom]])) The following instructions allow the player to farm [[Mycelium]] and increase the collection. Show Instructions Make a large dirt platform. Either do it in an area that is exposed to sunlight, or light up the surface. Mycelium needs a light level of at least 9 above it to spread to nearby dirt, and the dirt needs a light level of at least 4, so not everything needs to be at light level 9 or higher, but most of it should be. If you want to increase your Mycelium collection, plant a bunch of 2x2 trees on top of the dirt. Trees place dirt blocks under their trunks when they generate, and once they have been turned into Mycelium, these blocks will count towards the Mycelium collection. Add one or more Mycelium blocks for it to spread from. If you don't have any yet, use the Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]) to get some. Wait until the platform has finished turning into Mycelium. Use a shovel with [[Silk Touch]] to harvest the Mycelium. If you don't need to increase the collection, you can use a [[Block Zapper]] instead.|
|[[Nether Quartz]]|Dropped by Zombie Miners ([[Abyssal Miner]]) (requires Fishing XXIV (24) ([[Fishing]])). Produced by the [[Voidling Minion]] (requires Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])).|
|[[Netherrack]]|Obtained by Lava Fishing ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Obsidian]]|Obtained by using a pickaxe with a [[Breaking Power]] of at least 4 to mine Obsidian generated by putting a lava source in contact with water. Additional Water Buckets and Lava Buckets are available through Trades ([[Stranded]]). Obsidian can also be mined from the [[Nether Wart Island]] (requires Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]])). Dropped by Obsidian Defenders ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Red Sand]]|Purchased from a Villager Trade ([[Stranded]]) for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. The item can be purchased from the following Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]), but it does not count towards the collection. Trade: 1 [[Sand]] → 1 [[Red Sand]] (requires Sand II (2) ([[Sand]]))|
|[[Redstone Dust]]|Dropped by Redstone Pigmen ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Sand]]|Obtained from the [[Desert Island]]. Prior to reaching Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]]), the Desert Island can be obtained from Villagers ([[Stranded]]). Obtained from the [[Pond Island]] (requires Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])).|
|[[Sulphur]]|The Sulphur collection is not required to unlock the Builder. Purchased from a Villager Trade ([[Stranded]]) for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. Obtained by killing Corrupted Sea Creatures. The following instructions allow the player to get larger amounts of [[Sulphur]] and increase the collection. Show Instructions Lava fish to get Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]), which are guaranteed to drop from Corrupted ([[Corrupted Mobs]]) Sea Creatures. The following increase the chance to fish up Corrupted Sea Creatures. +0.1% per level from the [[Fishing]] skill. +1% per level from the [[Corruption]] Enchantment, which is obtained by killing corrupted mobs. +0.2% per tier from the Infection Attribute ([[Attributes]]), which has a 1/3 chance to be applied to a [[Magma Rod]] when it is crafted. When you reach Magmafish IV (4) ([[Magmafish]]) (1,000 items), you can craft a [[Magma Rod]] with 10 [[Silver Magmafish]]. Magma Rods require Fishing XXVII (27) ([[Fishing]]) to use. [[Attributes]] on rods can be combined using /attributemenu if you have an active [[Booster Cookie]]. When you reach Magmafish VI (6) ([[Magmafish]]) (15,000 items), you may want to make a Small Lava Fishing Sack ([[Lava Fishing Sack]]) with 8 [[Silver Magmafish]]. Continue once you have 20 Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]). Using the instructions for Mycelium above, reach Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]]) (10,000 items) and get 25,600 total Mycelium. Craft 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mycelium]]. Craft a [[Corrupt Soil]] with the 20 Corrupted Fragments and 160 Enchanted Mycelium. Place the Corrupt Soil in a Minion that generates mobs. Corrupt Soil makes the Minion generate [[Corrupted Mobs]], which lets it collect Sulphur and Corrupted Fragments. The amount of Sulphur and Corrupted Fragments depends on the amount of items the Minion collects, so a [[Slime Minion]] is the most efficient option. Once the Minion has collected 20 Corrupted Fragments, get another 160 Enchanted Mycelium and craft another Corrupt Soil. Keep making more Corrupt Soil for as many Minions as you like.|
[Combat Collections]
|[[Blaze Rod]]|Dropped by Blazes ([[Stranded]]). Dropped by Lava Blazes ([[Lava Blaze]]) from Lava Fishing ([[Stranded]]). Getting [[Blaze Powder]] from Lava Fishing ([[Stranded]]) increases the Blaze Rod collection, but Blaze Rods cannot be obtained this way.|
|[[Bone]]|Dropped by Skeletons ([[Stranded]]), Wolves ([[Stranded]]), and Old Wolves ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Chili Pepper]]|Obtained by putting LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] into an [[Inferno Minion]]. The following instructions explain how to do so in more detail. Show Instructions These instructions assume that you already have the necessary equipment for slaying Tier I Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]). ▦ Show Recipes First, gather the following materials and use them to craft 2 [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]]. Recipe: [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] x32/[[Enchanted Glowstone]] x4 + [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x8/[[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] + [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x32/[[Whipped Magma Cream]] x4 + [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x32/[[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] x4 + [[Enchanted Red Sand]] x8/[[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] + [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] x32/[[Mutant Nether Wart]] x4 → [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] x2/[[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] x40 8 [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 8 [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 32 [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] 32 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 32 [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 32 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] Slay Tier I Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]) until you have 256 (4 stacks) [[Derelict Ashe]]. Craft 2 [[Molten Powder]] using the [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] and [[Derelict Ashe]]. Recipe: [[Derelict Ashe]] x32 ×4 + [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] → [[Molten Powder]] Craft a [[Firedust Dagger]] using 30 [[Silver Magmafish]], 1 [[Molten Powder]], and a Stick. Recipe: [[Silver Magmafish]] x30 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → [[Firedust Dagger]] Craft a [[Twilight Dagger]] using 5 [[Enchanted Sulphur]], 1 [[Molten Powder]], and a Stick. Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x5 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → [[Twilight Dagger]] Using the daggers, slay Tier II Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]) until you have 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Derelict Ashe]]. Craft a [[Blaze Minion]] using 80 Blaze Rods ([[Blaze Rod]]) and a Wooden Sword. Recipe: [[Blaze Rod]] x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Sword]] → [[Blaze Minion]] Craft an [[Inferno Minion]] using the [[Blaze Minion]] and 80 [[Derelict Ashe]]. Recipe: [[Derelict Ashe]] x10 ×8 + [[Blaze Minion]] → [[Inferno Minion]] Keep slaying Tier II Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]). As you get more [[Derelict Ashe]], make more Inferno Minions. Except for Tier I, all tiers of the [[Inferno Demonlord]] have a chance to drop [[Distillates]]. The Tier II boss has a 21.1% chance to drop 2 matching Distillates. The Tier III boss has a 20.3% chance to drop a set of 6 to 10 matching Distillates. The Tier IV boss has a 19.2% chance to drop a set of 16 to 24 matching Distillates. Continue once you have 13,824 (216 stacks) [[Crude Gabagool]] from the Inferno Minions and 6 matching Distillates. Craft 604 (9 stacks plus 28) [[Sulphuric Coal]] using the following materials. You only need 601, but they are crafted 4 at a time. Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Coal]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sulphur]] → [[Sulphuric Coal]] x4/[[Sulphuric Coal]] x12 151 (2 stacks plus 23) [[Enchanted Sulphur]], which is equivalent to 24,160 (377 stacks plus 32) [[Sulphur]]. 2,416 (37 stacks plus 48) [[Enchanted Coal]], which would take 62 days to get using a single Tier XI [[Coal Minion]]. Craft 576 (9 stacks) [[Fuel Gabagool]] using 13,824 [[Crude Gabagool]] and 576 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Crude Gabagool]] x3/[[Sulphuric Coal]] ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]]/[[Very Crude Gabagool]] → [[Fuel Gabagool]]/[[Fuel Gabagool]] x8 Craft 24 [[Heavy Gabagool]] using 576 [[Fuel Gabagool]] and 24 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Fuel Gabagool]] x3 ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Heavy Gabagool]] Craft a [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] using 24 [[Heavy Gabagool]] and 1 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Heavy Gabagool]] x3 ×4 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] Buy 2 Inferno Fuel Blocks ([[Inferno Fuel Block]]) from the [[Bits Shop]] for 75 [[Bits]] each. Using the Inferno Fuel Blocks ([[Inferno Fuel Block]]), [[Hypergolic Gabagool]], and 6 matching [[Distillates]], craft a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]. Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] Put the LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] into one of your Inferno Minions ([[Inferno Minion]]). When using LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], Inferno Minions have a 1/156 chance per harvest to generate a [[Chili Pepper]]. On average, they will generate 10.05 Chili Peppers ([[Chili Pepper]]) from each LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], which each last 24 hours. There is a 0.0042% chance that they won't generate any Chili Peppers ([[Chili Pepper]]). If that somehow happens, you have to make another LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].|

|[[Ghast Tear]]|Dropped by Ghasts ([[Stranded]]).|

|[[Slimeball]]|Dropped by Slimes ([[Stranded]]).|

[Foraging Collections]
|[[Oak Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Oak Saplings, which can be obtained from the leaves of both the tree that starts on the island and trees grown from the saplings. Dropped by Pack Spirits ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Birch Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Birch Saplings, which can be obtained from the Birch Leaves in the bushes in front of the Barn in the Garden ([[The Garden]]), a Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]), and from the leaves of trees grown from the saplings. Trade: 2 [[Enchanted Oak Log]] → 1 Birch Sapling (requires Foraging II (2) ([[Foraging]])) Dropped by Pack Spirits ([[Stranded]]). Obtained while cleaning plots in the Garden ([[The Garden]]).|
|[[Spruce Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Spruce Saplings, which are obtained from a Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]) and from the leaves of trees grown from the saplings. There are also a few Spruce Leaves on some Garden ([[The Garden]]) plots, which have a chance to drop saplings. Trade: 4 [[Enchanted Birch Log]] → 1 Spruce Sapling (requires Foraging IV (4) ([[Foraging]])) Dropped by Howling Spirits ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Dark Oak Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Dark Oak Saplings, which are obtained from a Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]) and from the leaves of trees grown from the saplings. Trade: 6 [[Enchanted Spruce Log]] → 4 Dark Oak Sapling (requires Foraging VI (6) ([[Foraging]])) Dropped by Howling Spirits ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Acacia Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Acacia Saplings, which are obtained from a Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]) and from the leaves of trees grown from the saplings. Trade: 8 [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] → 1 Acacia Sapling (requires Foraging VIII (8) ([[Foraging]])) Dropped by Howling Spirits ([[Stranded]]).|
|[[Jungle Log]]|Harvested from trees grown from Jungle Saplings, which are obtained from a Stranded-only Trade ([[Stranded]]) and from the leaves of trees grown from the saplings. Trade: 10 [[Enchanted Acacia Log]] → 1 Jungle Sapling (requires Foraging X (10) ([[Foraging]])) Dropped by Soul of the Alpha ([[Stranded]]).|
[Fishing Collections]
|[[Raw Cod]]|Obtained by fishing manually or from using a [[Fishing Minion]].|
|[[Raw Salmon]]|(same as above)|
|[[Pufferfish]]|(same as above)|
|[[Tropical Fish]]|(same as above)|
|[[Sponge]]|(same as above)|
|[[Prismarine Crystals]]|Obtained by fishing manually or from using a [[Fishing Minion]]. Dropped by Sea Guardians ([[Sea Guardian]]), which can be fished up, but they also spawn naturally where the biome was changed with a Deep Ocean Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) (requires Ink Sac IV (4) ([[Ink Sac]])).|
|[[Prismarine Shard]]|(same as above)|
|[[Clay Ball]]|Obtained by fishing manually. The Fishing Minion does not produce Clay Balls. There is a Trade ([[Trades]]) for Clay Ball, but it doesn't increase the collection. The Trade is unlocked at Seeds II (2) ([[Seeds]]).|
|[[Ink Sac]]|Dropped by Squids ([[Squid]]) and Night Squids ([[Night Squid]]). Night Squids can only be caught when using Dark Bait ([[Bait]]) at night, and they drop on average 4.6 times as many Ink Sacs as Squids. The [[Squid Hat]] increases the chance of catching Squids.|
|[[Lily Pad]]|Dropped by Sea Guardians ([[Sea Guardian]]), which can be fished up, but they also spawn naturally where the biome was changed with a Deep Ocean Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) (requires Ink Sac IV (4) ([[Ink Sac]])). Dropped by various other [[Sea Creatures]].|
|[[Magmafish]]|Obtained by Lava Fishing ([[Stranded]]).|

# Strength Nerf
Strength Nerf was a change to the [[Damage Calculation]], which happened as a part of 0.11.5, which also introduced [[Enderman Slayer]].
## Details
Before this change, the [[Damage]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Strength]] interacted as follows:
- Damage=(5+WeaponDamage+Strength5)×(1+Strength100)×(1+CritDamage100)×(1+Additive Multiplier100)
The former 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] Minikloon, gave out these reasons for this change:
- Its multiplicative nature leads to a very large difference between low/midgame players and top-end players. This makes it challenging to create new combat content for a vast range of players.
- The strength conversion + multiplicative features makes it hard for players to gauge which weapon is stronger when looking at two weapons. It's also hard and delicate to tune your stats composition to deal the most damage.
Following this change the current, simplified version of the damage formula is this:
- Damage=(5+WeaponDamage)×(1+Strength100)×(1+CritDamage100)×(1+Additive Multiplier100)
## Calculator
The Calculator below can be used to calculate player's damage before and after the Strength Nerf.
calcpage  = Calculator:Strength Nerf
 template  = Template:Calculator/Strength Nerf
 form      = StrengthNerfCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = dmg|Damage|0|int
 param     = str|Strength|0|number
 param     = cd|Crit Damage|50|number
 param     = additiveDamage|Additive Damage Multiplier|0|int
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Trivia
- The formerly very popular armor set of 3/4 [[Strong Dragon Armor]] and [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] was much stronger before this change, as players could invest everything they could into [[Strength]] which was more effective than [[Crit Damage]], but would be improved further due to the [[Crit Damage]] granted via the Helmet's ability
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 26, 2021|Announced Strength Nerf as an upcoming change.|
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Introduced the Strength Nerf.|

# Sun Gecko Essence
Aliases: Sun Essence
[Sun Gecko Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Sun Gecko Essence]] Sun Gecko Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is used in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
[[Sun Gecko Essence]] Sun Gecko Essence can be obtained in [[The Mountaintop]] by defeating [[Sun Gecko]].

[[Sun Gecko Essence]] Sun Gecko Essence can be used in the Sun Gecko Essence Shop by talking to [[Oruo The Almighty]] in the [[Continuum]].

# Sword Types
Aliases: Combat Axes, Dagger, Daggers, Karambit, Karambit (disambiguation), Katana, Katana (disambiguation), Katanas, Scythes, Sword Type
Sword Types are a subcategorization of [[Swords]]. They are defined by the sword_type parameter in their NBT data.
The current sword types that exist are:
- Scythes
- Daggers
- Katanas
- Karambits
## Sword Types
|Name|value|
|-|-|
|Katana|KATANA|
|Dagger|DAGGER|
|Scythe|SCYTHE|
|Karambit|KARAMBIT|

- Currently, all Katanas are a part of [[Enderman Slayer]].
- The only Dagger that is not a [[Blaze Slayer]] weapon is the [[Livid Dagger]].
- [[Sinseeker Scythe]] was not set to Scythe type when Sword Types were added.

# Tal Ker/Dialogue
### First Time
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction 1|[NPC] Tal Ker: Hey!|
|Interaction 2|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm the guy that never stops talking|
|Interaction 3|[NPC] Tal Ker: All I do is say stuff|
|Interaction 4|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm not entirely sure why but I can't help it|
|Interaction 5|[NPC] Tal Ker: There really is no point talking to me|
|Interaction 6|[NPC] Tal Ker: I won't give you anything|
|Interaction 7|[NPC] Tal Ker: I won't even say anything interesting|
|Interaction 8|[NPC] Tal Ker: I just talk about random things|
|Interaction 9|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like for example|
|Interaction 10|[NPC] Tal Ker: The other day I met some kind of wizard|
|Interaction 11|[NPC] Tal Ker: And they told me to come back in 3 to 5 business days|
|Interaction 12|[NPC] Tal Ker: That was over a year ago|
|Interaction 13|[NPC] Tal Ker: Still nothing|
|Interaction 14|[NPC] Tal Ker: What kind of people do that?|
|Interaction 15|[NPC] Tal Ker: Tells you to come back in a few days for no reason|
|Interaction 16|[NPC] Tal Ker: Really makes you think|
|Interaction 17|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also this guy, Crappox or something|
|Interaction 18|[NPC] Tal Ker: Told me to kill enemy monsters for him|
|Interaction 19|[NPC] Tal Ker: I asked him how much he'd pay me|
|Interaction 20|[NPC] Tal Ker: He said I had to pay him instead|
|Interaction 21|[NPC] Tal Ker: Makes no sense to me|
|Interaction 22|[NPC] Tal Ker: I met a guy called Jerry too|
|Interaction 23|[NPC] Tal Ker: Smart guy, he's going places|
|Interaction 24|[NPC] Tal Ker: Met him a few times in fact, he's always around|
|Interaction 25|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not the best for conversations but|
|Interaction 26|[NPC] Tal Ker: At least he's a good listener|
|Interaction 27|[NPC] Tal Ker: And I like that, good listeners|
|Interaction 28|[NPC] Tal Ker: But you know what I don't like listening to?|
|Interaction 29|[NPC] Tal Ker: People who play that harp|
|Interaction 30|[NPC] Tal Ker: You know the one I'm talking about?|
|Interaction 31|[NPC] Tal Ker: Always people playing it, but terrible music|
|Interaction 32|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes they even stop in the middle of the song|
|Interaction 33|[NPC] Tal Ker: And they restart, just like that|
|Interaction 34|[NPC] Tal Ker: Go figures|
|Interaction 35|[NPC] Tal Ker: There's also this guy who's asking me to race him sometimes|
|Interaction 36|[NPC] Tal Ker: He's acting like he's the best|
|Interaction 37|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I always beat him first try|
|Interaction 38|[NPC] Tal Ker: What's his problem?|
|Interaction 39|[NPC] Tal Ker: At least he's an original|
|Interaction 40|[NPC] Tal Ker: Most people always talk about coins around here|
|Interaction 41|[NPC] Tal Ker: Everything has a value, and time is money|
|Interaction 42|[NPC] Tal Ker: People don't take their time to enjoy the little things|
|Interaction 43|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even you I'm sure|
|Interaction 44|[NPC] Tal Ker: You really think I'm going to reward you with something|
|Interaction 45|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I'm not, I swear|
|Interaction 46|[NPC] Tal Ker: You can leave at any time and it won't affect anything|
|Interaction 47|[NPC] Tal Ker: I just like to talk, I can go on for hours|
|Interaction 48|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone told me I was interesting once|
|Interaction 49|[NPC] Tal Ker: Didn't believe them one bit|
|Interaction 50|[NPC] Tal Ker: So many people lying and trying to be your friend|
|Interaction 51|[NPC] Tal Ker: They all have a motive|
|Interaction 52|[NPC] Tal Ker: Especially those mayor candidates|
|Interaction 53|[NPC] Tal Ker: All they want is your vote|
|Interaction 54|[NPC] Tal Ker: But do they really care about me?|
|Interaction 55|[NPC] Tal Ker: Doubt [x]|
|Interaction 56|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's always so black and white with them|
|Interaction 57|[NPC] Tal Ker: Mining, fishing, they never mix|
|Interaction 58|[NPC] Tal Ker: Why is that? What if I like mining and fishing?|
|Interaction 59|[NPC] Tal Ker: But there is no point arguing|
|Interaction 60|[NPC] Tal Ker: They are always right|
|Interaction 61|[NPC] Tal Ker: And the people, sheep I say|
|Interaction 62|[NPC] Tal Ker: They always vote for the same|
|Interaction 63|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's always a landslide|
|Interaction 64|[NPC] Tal Ker: Everyone wants to do like everyone else|
|Interaction 65|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not me, I'm a free spirit|
|Interaction 66|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'll tell you why|
|Interaction 67|[NPC] Tal Ker: My home is clean and beautiful|
|Interaction 68|[NPC] Tal Ker: Is your island beautiful?|
|Interaction 69|[NPC] Tal Ker: A proper island is the key to success|
|Interaction 70|[NPC] Tal Ker: Healthy island leads to healthy life|
|Interaction 71|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also, remember those island we could place?|
|Interaction 72|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like the sand one and forest one?|
|Interaction 73|[NPC] Tal Ker: What happened to those, why not more?|
|Interaction 74|[NPC] Tal Ker: So weird, it looked promising|
|Interaction 75|[NPC] Tal Ker: Instead you have me|
|Interaction 76|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I wonder|
|Interaction 77|[NPC] Tal Ker: If I wasn't talking, what would people do instead|
|Interaction 78|[NPC] Tal Ker: Am I a waste of time and space|
|Interaction 79|[NPC] Tal Ker: Is that why I'm here, on this world|
|Interaction 80|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's time I do something with my life|
|Interaction 81|[NPC] Tal Ker: I could run for mayor|
|Interaction 82|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder if people would vote for me|
|Interaction 83|[NPC] Tal Ker: What would my program even be?|
|Interaction 84|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would reward people for talking|
|Interaction 85|[NPC] Tal Ker: But not those who talk about their auctions, yuck|
|Interaction 86|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would reward people who explore and walk around|
|Interaction 87|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would fill the village with friends with amazing stories to tell|
|Interaction 88|[NPC] Tal Ker: But who would vote for me|
|Interaction 89|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe instead I should stay there and keep talking|
|Interaction 90|[NPC] Tal Ker: Talking is my favorite event|
|Interaction 91|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like events but I prefer uneventful days|
|Interaction 92|[NPC] Tal Ker: That way I can keep talking|
|Interaction 93|[NPC] Tal Ker: I can't believe you are still listening|
|Interaction 94|[NPC] Tal Ker: You are one of a kind|
|Interaction 95|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have many more stories up my sleeve|
|Interaction 96|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like that time I found a fairy soul|
|Interaction 97|[NPC] Tal Ker: I went to Tia after because she asked me to|
|Interaction 98|[NPC] Tal Ker: So I asked her, what are fairy souls?|
|Interaction 99|[NPC] Tal Ker: And she said they are the remains of dead fairies|
|Interaction 100|[NPC] Tal Ker: Isn't that terrible?! So many dead fairies everywhere!|
|Interaction 101|[NPC] Tal Ker: She also said that all those fairies didn't die a peaceful death.|
|Interaction 102|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were all killed! A long time ago.|
|Interaction 103|[NPC] Tal Ker: But why would anyone do that? And who did it?|
|Interaction 104|[NPC] Tal Ker: The strangest part is, Tia really didn't seem phased about it.|
|Interaction 105|[NPC] Tal Ker: Just thinking about it I have a chill down my spine.|
|Interaction 106|[NPC] Tal Ker: Those fairies are so strange.|
|Interaction 107|[NPC] Tal Ker: But you know what else is strange?|
|Interaction 108|[NPC] Tal Ker: Accessories, I mean what are those|
|Interaction 109|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have an entire bag full of accessories|
|Interaction 110|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm basically a garage sale at this point|
|Interaction 111|[NPC] Tal Ker: My favorite one is the cat one|
|Interaction 112|[NPC] Tal Ker: I love cats|
|Interaction 113|[NPC] Tal Ker: I want a cat pet but not the black one, bad memories|
|Interaction 114|[NPC] Tal Ker: Cats are the best, they listen to me|
|Interaction 115|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think they are learning, one day they will take control|
|Interaction 116|[NPC] Tal Ker: But be my guest, a cat mayor is all I need|
|Interaction 117|[NPC] Tal Ker: My second favorite activity is sleeping|
|Interaction 118|[NPC] Tal Ker: But even in my sleep, I dream of talking|
|Interaction 119|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I dream that I'm talking to myself|
|Interaction 120|[NPC] Tal Ker: A whole table with 10 clones of me around it|
|Interaction 121|[NPC] Tal Ker: All of us pretending to be more interesting than the others|
|Interaction 122|[NPC] Tal Ker: But how could we? We are all the same|
|Interaction 123|[NPC] Tal Ker: I guess my dreams are not very creative|
|Interaction 124|[NPC] Tal Ker: I don't have a great imagination, that's why I never lie|
|Interaction 125|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder though, do you think there will be another world like this one day?|
|Interaction 126|[NPC] Tal Ker: Somewhere else, not exactly the same, but better in some way|
|Interaction 127|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also it sounds crazy but, do you think we could exist without cubes?|
|Interaction 128|[NPC] Tal Ker: Ender Pearls are my favorite items|
|Interaction 129|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not because of what they do, but because they are circles|
|Interaction 130|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wish the world was a circle too, it's so much prettier|
|Interaction 131|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'd love to be in a circle world|
|Interaction 132|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe there is a circle world somewhere, with someone just like me|
|Interaction 133|[NPC] Tal Ker: In their circle world, dreaming of a cubic world|
|Interaction 134|[NPC] Tal Ker: You really are a great listener|
|Interaction 135|[NPC] Tal Ker: Now I regret saying I wouldn't reward you|
|Interaction 136|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I swore, and I am a man of my words|
|Interaction 137|[NPC] Tal Ker: But thank you for listening, I appreciate it a lot|
|Interaction 138|[NPC] Tal Ker: I hope that you are happy and that everything is good in your life|
|Interaction 139|[NPC] Tal Ker: There are ups and down sometimes but there is always a shinning light|
|Interaction 140|[NPC] Tal Ker: My shinning light is you, a beam of hope in this dark cave|
|Interaction 141|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'll tell you the story of my life|
|Interaction 142|[NPC] Tal Ker: Once upon a time, on an island far away|
|Interaction 143|[NPC] Tal Ker: I used to live in a small town, we had a house, 2 floors and an attic|
|Interaction 144|[NPC] Tal Ker: Back then I was quite shy and reserved|
|Interaction 145|[NPC] Tal Ker: I liked reading and listening to birds|
|Interaction 146|[NPC] Tal Ker: There was always a cat around the house, sometimes killing the birds|
|Interaction 147|[NPC] Tal Ker: I hated that cat with a passion, but my parents seemed to enjoy its presence|
|Interaction 148|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day the cat gave birth to 3 little devils, and decided to hide them in our attic|
|Interaction 149|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were making so much noise, right above my room|
|Interaction 150|[NPC] Tal Ker: I never understood the reason we had to leave them be, I wanted them out|
|Interaction 151|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day after sundown, a fire started spreading in our house|
|Interaction 152|[NPC] Tal Ker: Quickly we understood it was too late to stop it, and exited the house with our valuables|
|Interaction 153|[NPC] Tal Ker: I was asked if I knew whether or not the kittens were still inside the house|
|Interaction 154|[NPC] Tal Ker: When asked that question, I remained silent, but I knew the answer|
|Interaction 155|[NPC] Tal Ker: Soon after, the flames were engulfing the house|
|Interaction 156|[NPC] Tal Ker: Suddenly the mom cat appeared from the shadows of the night and ran into the house|
|Interaction 157|[NPC] Tal Ker: This creature usually so afraid of the fire, rushed into the infernal flames|
|Interaction 158|[NPC] Tal Ker: About 20 seconds later, the cat passed the front door again, holding one of their cubs|
|Interaction 159|[NPC] Tal Ker: And immediately after, dashed back inside the fire to save the rest of their litter|
|Interaction 160|[NPC] Tal Ker: After the second trip, the cat was burning, but still went back in, right after making sure the cub was safe|
|Interaction 161|[NPC] Tal Ker: I waited 30 seconds, my eyes fixed on the front door, holding my breath|
|Interaction 162|[NPC] Tal Ker: Finally it came out with the last kitten|
|Interaction 163|[NPC] Tal Ker: The cat was in terrible shape, but the kittens were safe|
|Interaction 164|[NPC] Tal Ker: It looked one last time at them, and died next to the 3 little bodies|
|Interaction 165|[NPC] Tal Ker: I was in shambles, never in my life had I been so wrong|
|Interaction 166|[NPC] Tal Ker: Later on I buried the cat under my favorite tree, and adopted their cubs|
|Interaction 167|[NPC] Tal Ker: I loved those 3 little devils with all my heart and gave them all the care in the world|
|Interaction 168|[NPC] Tal Ker: They became beautiful and happy cats, their mom would have been proud|
|Interaction 169|[NPC] Tal Ker: This event changed me to my very core, everything about me had to change|
|Interaction 170|[NPC] Tal Ker: From that day I never hated anything anymore, and never stopped talking|
|Interaction 171|[NPC] Tal Ker: But having said this story, I'll exceptionally need to take a break|
|Interaction 172|[NPC] Tal Ker: I haven't told that story to many people|
|Interaction 173|[NPC] Tal Ker: Thank you for listening to me|

### Second Time
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction 1 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Oh you came back!|
|Interaction 2 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: For a moment I thought you wouldn't|
|Interaction 3 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sorry if I spooked you a little bit last time|
|Interaction 4 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I get carried away too much and tell personal stories|
|Interaction 5 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I apologize|
|Interaction 6 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You can call me Tal, some people call me TK|
|Interaction 7 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I don't actually live there, I'm just visiting|
|Interaction 8 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe one day I’ll show you my place|
|Interaction 9 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think I had dwarves somewhere in my family tree|
|Interaction 10 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That's why I look like one|
|Interaction 11 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like visiting islands, some more than others|
|Interaction 12 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Golden Mines, Crimson Isle... haven't been there in ages|
|Interaction 13 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I used to go to the End a lot, not anymore|
|Interaction 14 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The dragon heart corrupts everything, even the people|
|Interaction 15 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like it better down here, there are people like you|
|Interaction 16 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The story of these mines is old, older than the End island for sure!|
|Interaction 17 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You do know the story of the End island right?|
|Interaction 18 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Hum, well that's awkward|
|Interaction 19 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I guess they don't really teach you much do they|
|Interaction 20 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Unfortunately I don't know enough details myself|
|Interaction 21 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I do know a few things, you remember the giant skeleton on the island nearby?|
|Interaction 22 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The heart of that creature is sealed on the End island|
|Interaction 23 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not many people are left, who can tell the story of that day|
|Interaction 24 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe Jerry saw it, when he was very young|
|Interaction 25 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: And even he was probably born much later|
|Interaction 26 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The Calamity is the reason why the earth got shattered into many islands|
|Interaction 27 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wish I knew more, it's a fantastic subject|
|Interaction 28 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The end of an era, and the beginning of a much darker one|
|Interaction 29 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sorry if I'm boring you, I like history|
|Interaction 30 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I went to the Sky Academy, greater teachers there|
|Interaction 31 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You should go there someday, even greater adventurers need to learn a few things!|
|Interaction 32 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: My parents always told me to focus on my studies|
|Interaction 33 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were right, I'm a successful young man now!|
|Interaction 34 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe not so young, but my point still stands|
|Interaction 35 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Studies on one side and fun on the other, both are very important|
|Interaction 36 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Yin and yang, one should not go without the other|
|Interaction 37 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The Academy was tough, but I met a few friends there|
|Interaction 38 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You know the banker?|
|Interaction 39 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He was in my promotion back then|
|Interaction 40 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He invented the bank!|
|Interaction 41 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I still have no idea where all that interest is coming from|
|Interaction 42 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A genius this guy|
|Interaction 43 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like seeing the world evolve through time|
|Interaction 44 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: What they did to the village a few years ago was great|
|Interaction 45 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I feel like we entered the modern age|
|Interaction 46 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The technology is progressing fast, who knows what's coming next!|
|Interaction 47 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sure one day people will fly to the moon|
|Interaction 48 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I knew a time without adventurers, without banks and auctions|
|Interaction 49 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not much was happening back then|
|Interaction 50 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: And then it happened|
|Interaction 51 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day hordes of people appeared out of nowhere|
|Interaction 52 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Nobody seems to know why|
|Interaction 53 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have my theory but I won't tell you|
|Interaction 54 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You would think I'm crazy, maybe you already do|
|Interaction 55 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: All those people at once|
|Interaction 56 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It was really overwhelming|
|Interaction 57 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It did create so many business opportunities|
|Interaction 58 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The economy is booming|
|Interaction 59 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That guy, Bazaar, he really took advantage of it|
|Interaction 60 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Bazaar isn't even his real name|
|Interaction 61 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He changed his name to Bazaar because it was better for business, he said|
|Interaction 62 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A real specimen|
|Interaction 63 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Lots of people think they understand the market|
|Interaction 64 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I gave up on it|
|Interaction 65 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I did buy some stocks of stonks when he opened|
|Interaction 66 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I still have a few|
|Interaction 67 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That Warren guy made a lot of money and disappeared|
|Interaction 68 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Another shark|
|Interaction 69 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe I should sell my own currency and make people believe it's worth many coins|
|Interaction 70 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like Talcoins?|
|Interaction 71 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The value of anything is in the eyes of the buyers|
|Interaction 72 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sure I could raise the price of anything if I really tried|
|Interaction 73 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people do that all the time in the community|
|Interaction 74 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like it though, it creates opportunities for everyone|
|Interaction 75 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think a lot of what happens around here can serve as a life lesson|
|Interaction 76 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's important to experience the right things when you are young|
|Interaction 77 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even bad moments can be blessings in disguise|
|Interaction 78 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone scammed me a very valuable thing one day|
|Interaction 79 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: They even got away with it, awful|
|Interaction 80 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: But never again have I gotten fooled|
|Interaction 81 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If I could I would pay him double|
|Interaction 82 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A lesson for a few coins, a great deal|
|Interaction 83 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Always stay vigilant|
|Interaction 84 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people don't see it the same way|
|Interaction 85 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Do you know the origin of the word utopia?|
|Interaction 86 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It means a place that does not exist|
|Interaction 87 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even the people who created the word knew it was a paradox|
|Interaction 88 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people are smarter than hundreds of other combined|
|Interaction 89 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Do you think they were born that way, or became?|
|Interaction 90 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have my own view on the matter|
|Interaction 91 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: My opinion is that it's a little bit of both|
|Interaction 92 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The smartest baby would become a monkey if it wasn't taught anything|
|Interaction 93 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone told me a story one day|
|Interaction 94 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Bird eggs are grey and dirty, snake eggs are white and clean|
|Interaction 95 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The bird decided to sit on the clean egg instead of his|
|Interaction 96 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The egg hatched and the snake ate the bird|
|Interaction 97 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He told me the moral of the story is that birds are stupid|
|Interaction 98 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: But is that the moral of the story?|
|Interaction 99 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about appearances being deceiving|
|Interaction 100 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A clean shell can hide a dirty mind|
|Interaction 101 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about beauty being subjective|
|Interaction 102 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If the bird found dirty eggs pretty, he wouldn't have gotten in that situation|
|Interaction 103 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about planning ahead|
|Interaction 104 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Short term comfort can lead to troubles in the future|
|Interaction 105 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about trusting your family more than others|
|Interaction 106 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If you tell the same story to multiple people they might understand it differently|
|Interaction 107 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: What do you think is the moral of the story?|
|Interaction 108 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I love talking, but I also think a lot!|
|Interaction 109 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm going to think for a bit, thanks for listening to me! See you!|

# Team Treasurite
Aliases: Team Treasurite Member
Team Treasurite is a collection of five different [[Mob|Mobs]] in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]]. These include [[Grunt]], [[Executive Sebastian]], [[Executive Wendy]], [[Executive Viper]], and [[Boss Corleone]].
## Spawning
Each mob can be found in various parts of the [[Mithril Deposits]], often grouped together with other members. Spawned mob is decided by a weighted list:
|Mob|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|[[Grunt]]|100|76.34% (1 in 1.31)|
|[[Executive Sebastian]]|10|7.63% (1 in 13.1)|
|[[Executive Wendy]]|10|7.63% (1 in 13.1)|
|[[Executive Viper]]|10|7.63% (1 in 13.1)|
|[[Boss Corleone]]|1|0.76% (1 in 131)|
[[Boss Corleone]] additionally has two dedicated spawn locations within the [[Mithril Deposits]].
## Behavior
Each mob throws a ball when in combat which spawns a creature based off the mob who threw it, and white text above their head will display: [mob] go!.
## Mobs

|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|[Lv62] [[Executive Sebastian]]|[[Health]]: 18,000; [[Damage]]: 450|[[Mithril Deposits]]|[[Humanoid]]|8–15x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8–15x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Treasurite]]|+93 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 20 [[Coins]]; 68 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv62] [[Executive Wendy]]|[[Health]]: 18,000; [[Damage]]: 450|[[Mithril Deposits]]|[[Humanoid]]|8–15x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8–15x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Treasurite]]|+93 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 20 [[Coins]]; 68 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv62] [[Executive Viper]]|[[Health]]: 18,000; [[Damage]]: 450|[[Mithril Deposits]]|[[Humanoid]]|8–15x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8–15x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Treasurite]]|+93 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 20 [[Coins]]; 68 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv195] [[Boss Corleone]]|[[Health]]: 1,000,000; [[Damage]]: 4,000|[[Mithril Deposits]]|[[Humanoid]] [[Subterranean]]|18–41x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 15–27x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Corleonite]]|+292 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 65 [[Coins]]; 214 [[Experience]]|

## Trivia
- Executive Sebastian and Belle are most likely based off the French film, Belle and Sebastian, where a boy befriends a wild dog and names the dog, "Belle", or beautiful.
- Boss Corleone is likely based off Vito Corleone from Mario Puzo's 1969 novel, The Godfather.
The ball thrown by Team Treasurite members to summon their associated creature
- The mobs are based on Team Rocket from Pokémon.
  - The ball that each mob throws in battle resembles Dive Ball in Pokémon.
  - The Grunt looks like a Team Rocket Grunt, Executive Sebastian looks like Proton, Executive Wendy looks like Ariana, and Executive Viper is most likely Archer. Corleone, as the leader, is probably the counterpart of Giovanni. Within Pokémon, Executives are the highest ranking members of Team Rocket after Giovanni.
  - The Grunt's [[Bestiary]] entry, "Team Treasurite is up to no good. Prepare for trouble!" references Jessie and James' catchphrase originating from the Pokémon anime series.
