# The Breach
Aliases: Breach, Close the Breach, Close the breach, The Disturbance, The breach
The Breach, sometimes also referred to as The Rift, or Riftway by [[Nicole]], is a tear located atop the [[Mountain]] on the [[Hub Island]], as well as in [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], created during the [[Jythar|Calamity]].
## Lore
After [[Jythar]] used the [[Fels|Fel Magic]], The Breach has appeared on the place he was trapped. The Breach was later studied by many, including [[Dr. Emmett]], who was accidentally trapped in the [[Rift Dimension]] because of it. The Breach causes time distortion nearby. The [[Wizard Tower|Wizard Portal]] was built close to the [[Wilderness]] due to [[Tia the Fairy|the fairy's]] aura stabilizing the distortion. [[Arba]], [[Arbadak]] and [[Mazakala]] theorize how The Breach could be used for time travel, which is correct as [[The Watcher]] uses it to send visions to his younger self to become him.
## Mechanics
The player can venture through [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] to close The Breach. Closing The Breach requires 35m00s [[Rift Time]] after completing [[Wizardman]]'s trials and obtaining the [[Wizard Face]].
## Trivia
- After closing The Breach the [[Player]] is teleported out of the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], onto top of the [[Mountain]], granting them the [[Achievements|"The first to have ever done it" Achievement]] and triggering a dialogue with [[Wizard]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added The Breach and NPCs that talk about it|
|Feb 16, 2023|Added the Dungeon Journals ([[Journal]]), giving more information about it.|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|The player can now close The Breach.|

# The Fish Family
Aliases: Fish Family, The Fish, The Fish (disambiguation)
The Fish are various [[Collectable Items]] obtainable by various means in the game.
## Usage
Showing The Fish to [[Coral]] will reward the player and making The Fish [[Soulbound|co-op soulbound]]. Note that although [[Chill the Fish|Chill]], [[Egg the Fish|Egg]], [[Oops the Fish|Oops]], and [[Spook the Fish|Spook]] count as part of The Fish Family, [[Coral]] does not reward the player for showing those obtained from fishing in the Main Lobby of Hypixel.
## List
|Item|Name|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|
|[[Bat the Fish]]|[[Bat the Fish]]|A 2% drop from a [[Bat Piñata]]|

# Token of the Forest
Aliases: Token Of The Forest, Tokens of the Forest
[Token of the Forest]
Obtained Via: Heart of the Forest Center of the Forest
A Token of the Forest is currency used to unlock perks in the [[Heart of the Forest]].
## Obtaining
The players can obtain a maximum amount of 15 Tokens of the Forest via leveling up the [[Heart of the Forest]]. An additional 2 Tokens of the Forest can be unlocked with [[Forest Whispers]] via leveling the [[Heart of the Forest|Center of the Forest]] to Level 1 and 5.
|Obtain Source|Received Amount|Total|
|-|-|-|
|Heart of the Forest I (1) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|1|1|
|Heart of the Forest II (2) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|3|
|Heart of the Forest III (3) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|5|
|Heart of the Forest IV (4) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|7|
|Heart of the Forest V (5) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|9|
|Center of the Forest Level 1|1|10|
|Heart of the Forest VI (6) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|12|
|Heart of the Forest VII (7) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|14|
|Center of the Forest Level 5|1|15|
|Heart of the Forest VIII (8) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2|17|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Token of the Forest.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Token of the Forest.|

# Token of the Mountain
Aliases: Token Of The Mountain, Tokens of the Mountain
[Token of the Mountain]
Obtained Via: Heart of the Mountain Rhys
Token of the Mountain is a currency that players can use to unlock more perks of their [[Heart of the Mountain]].
## Obtaining
Players can obtain a maximum amount of 25 Tokens of the Mountain, via tiering up their [[Heart of the Mountain]]. Players receive their first Token of the Mountain when completing the quest for [[Rhys]] who gives [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain I (1)]]. Players can unlock 2 Tokens of the Mountain with [[Mithril Powder]] via leveling Core of the Mountain to Level 1 and 10.
